// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;

--- common/hash.hpp ---
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

--- results_output.hpp ---
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

    19	static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;
    20	
    21	// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
    22	struct Point {
    23	    double x, y;
    24	};
    25	
    26	// Structure to represent a cluster
    27	struct Cluster {
    28	    std::vector<int> members;
    29	    int seed_point;
    30	};
    31	
    32	// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
    33	void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    34	    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    35	    
    36	    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    37	    int count = 0;
    38	    
    39	    while (count < N) {
    40	        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
    41	        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
    42	        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
    43	        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
    44	        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
    45	        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
    46	        
    47	        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
    48	        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
    49	            group_cnt = N - count;
    50	        }
    51	        
    52	        while (group_cnt > 0) {
    53	            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
    54	            const double r = frand() * R;
    55	            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
    56	            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
    57	            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
    58	            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
    59	            
    60	            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
    61	                continue;
    62	            }
    63	            
    64	            points[count] = {x, y};
    65	            count++;
    66	            group_cnt--;
    67	        }
    68	    }
    69	}
    70	
    71	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
    72	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    73	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    74	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    75	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    76	}
    77	
    78	// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
    79	// Returns -1 if no such point exists
    80	int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
    81	                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
    82	                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
    83	                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
    84	                     const double threshold,
    85	                     const int point_count) {
    86	    int closest_point = -1;
    87	    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    88	    
    89	    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    90	    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
    91	        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
    92	        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
    93	        
    94	        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
    95	        double max_dist = 0.0;
    96	        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
    97	            const int member = cluster_members[i];
    98	            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
    99	            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
   100	        }
   101	        
   102	        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
   103	        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
   104	            min_diameter = max_dist;
   105	            closest_point = candidate;
   106	        }
   107	    }
   108	    
   109	    return closest_point;
   110	}
   111	
   112	// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
   113	// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
   114	int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
   115	                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
   116	                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
   117	                              const double threshold,
   118	                              const int point_count,
   119	                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
   120	    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
   121	    std::vector<int> members;
   122	    
   123	    // Add seed point
   124	    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
   125	    members.push_back(seed_point);
   126	    
   127	    // Iteratively add closest points
   128	    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
   129	        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
   130	        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
   131	                                             threshold, point_count);
   132	        
   133	        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
   134	        
   135	        in_cluster[closest] = true;
   136	        members.push_back(closest);
   137	    }
   138	    
   139	    // Copy members if requested
   140	    if (cluster_members) {
   141	        *cluster_members = members;
   142	    }
   143	    
   144	    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
   145	}
   146	
   147	// Main QT clustering algorithm
   148	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   149	                                  const double threshold) {
   150	    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   151	    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
   152	    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
   153	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   154	    
   155	    // Initialize unclustered indices
   156	    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
   157	        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
   158	    }
   159	    
   160	    // Main clustering loop
   161	    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
   162	        int max_cardinality = -1;
   163	        int best_seed = -1;
   164	        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
   165	        
   166	        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
   167	        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
   168	            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
   169	            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
   170	            
   171	            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
   172	            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
   173	                                                       threshold, N, 
   174	                                                       &candidate_members);
   175	            
   176	            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
   177	                max_cardinality = cardinality;
   178	                best_seed = seed;
   179	                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
   180	            }
   181	        }
   182	        
   183	        // If we found a cluster, add it
   184	        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
   185	            Cluster cluster;
   186	            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
   187	            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
   188	            clusters.push_back(cluster);
   189	            
   190	            // Mark all members as clustered
   191	            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
   192	                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
   193	            }
   194	            
   195	            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
   196	            unclustered_indices.erase(
   197	                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
   198	                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
   199	                unclustered_indices.end()
   200	            );
   201	        } else {
   202	            // No more clusters can be formed
   203	            break;
   204	        }
   205	    }
   206	    
   207	    return clusters;
   208	}
   209	
   210	// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
   211	bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
   212	                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
   213	                     const double threshold) {
   214	    bool valid = true;
   215	    
   216	    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
   217	    
   218	    // Check each cluster
   219	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   220	        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
   221	        double max_diameter = 0.0;
   222	        
   223	        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
   224	        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
   225	            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
   226	                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
   227	                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
   228	                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
   229	            }
   230	        }
   231	        
   232	        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
   233	            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
   234	                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
   235	        }
   236	        
   237	        // Validate diameter is within threshold
   238	        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
   239	            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
   240	                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
   241	            valid = false;
   242	        }
   243	    }
   244	    
   245	    // Check for duplicate memberships
   246	    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
   247	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   248	        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
   249	            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
   250	            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
   251	                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
   252	                       member, membership[member], c);
   253	                valid = false;
   254	            }
   255	            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
   256	        }
   257	    }
   258	    
   259	    // Count clustered points
   260	    int clustered_count = 0;
   261	    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
   262	        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
   263	    }
   264	    
   265	    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
   266	           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
   267	    
   268	    return valid;
   269	}
   270	
   271	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   272	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   273	    printf("Options:\n");
   274	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
   275	    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
   276	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   277	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   278	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   279	}
   280	
   281	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   282	    int num_points = 1000;
   283	    double threshold = 2.0;
   284	    bool validate = false;
   285	    bool printResults = false;
   286	    
   287	    // Parse command line arguments
   288	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   289	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   290	            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
   291	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   292	            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
   293	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   294	            validate = true;
   295	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   296	            printResults = true;
   297	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   298	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   299	            return 0;
   300	        } else {
   301	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   302	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   303	            return 1;
   304	        }
   305	    }
   306	    
   307	    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
   308	        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
   309	               num_points, threshold);
   310	        return 1;
   311	    }
   312	    
   313	    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
   314	    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
   315	    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
   316	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   317	    
   318	    // Generate synthetic data
   319	    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
   320	    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
   321	    
   322	    // Perform QT clustering
   323	    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   324	    
   325	    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
   326	    
   327	    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   328	    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
   329	        cluster_end - cluster_start);
   330	    
   331	    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
   332	    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
   333	    
   334	    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
   335	    int total_clustered = 0;
   336	    int max_cluster_size = 0;
   337	    
   338	    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
   339	        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
   340	        total_clustered += size;
   341	        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
   342	    }
   343	    
   344	    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
   345	        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
   346	    
   347	    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
   348	           total_clustered, num_points, 
   349	           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
   350	    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
   351	    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
   352	    
   353	    // Performance metrics
   354	    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
   355	    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
   356	    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
   357	    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
   358	           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
   359	    
   360	    // Print results for external validation
   361	    if (printResults) {
   362	        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
   363	        std::vector<double> membershipData;
   364	        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
   365	        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
   366	        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   367	            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
   368	                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
   369	            }
   370	        }
   371	        for (int m : membership) {
   372	            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
   373	        }
   374	        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
   375	    }
   376	    
   377	    // Validation
   378	    if (validate) {
   379	        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
   380	        
   381	        if (valid) {
   382	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   383	            return 0;
   384	        } else {
   385	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   386	            return 1;
   387	        }
   388	    }
   389	    
   390	    return 0;
   391	}

codex
The dominant cost is the independent candidate-cluster construction for every currently unclustered seed. The outer clustering decision is inherently iterative, so I’ll keep that ordering and parallelize the seed evaluations with deterministic tie-breaking; each worker will use private candidate/member storage, and the selected cluster will still be committed serially in the original order.

         in_cluster[closest] = true;
-        members.push_back(closest);
+        cluster_members.push_back(closest);
     }
     
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
-    }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    return static_cast<int>(cluster_members.size());

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
     
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<std::vector<int>> thread_best_members(max_threads);
+    bool done = false;
+
+    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
+    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent. Keep one OpenMP team
+    // alive for the whole algorithm to avoid repeatedly creating teams and to
+    // retain each worker's scratch buffers between iterations.
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, clustered, \
+                                               unclustered_indices, clusters, \
+                                               thread_best_cardinality, \
+                                               thread_best_position, \
+                                               thread_best_members, done)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);
+        std::vector<int> candidate_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = -1;
+
+            // Try each unclustered point as a seed. Dynamic scheduling balances
+            // seeds whose candidate clusters require different amounts of work.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size()); ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
+                    candidate_members, in_cluster);
+
+                // The serial implementation keeps the first seed on a tie.
+                // Compare the original position explicitly because dynamic
+                // scheduling does not define the order in which work arrives.
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality &&
+                     (local_best_position < 0 || i < local_best_position))) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                    thread_best_members[thread_id] = candidate_members;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_max_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp for makes every worker's
+            // result available before the deterministic serial reduction.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int max_cardinality = -1;
+                int best_position = -1;
+                int best_seed = -1;
+                std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+                for (int i = 0; i < max_threads; ++i) {
+                    const int position = thread_best_position[i];
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[i] == max_cardinality &&
+                         position >= 0 &&
+                         (best_position < 0 || position < best_position))) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[i];
+                        best_position = position;
+                        best_seed = position >= 0 ? unclustered_indices[position] : -1;
+                        best_cluster_members = thread_best_members[i];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. This is intentionally kept
+                // serial: it is the state transition that defines the next
+                // QT iteration and preserves the original semantics.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                           return clustered[index] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp single publishes the
+            // state transition before any worker starts the next iteration.
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

codex
The OpenMP implementation is now in place: a persistent team evaluates seeds with dynamic load balancing, retains per-thread scratch buffers, and reduces winners by original seed position so ties remain deterministic. I also added an exact early-rejection optimization in the distance scan and replaced `vector<bool>` flags with byte flags to reduce proxy/access overhead.

 exited 1 in 0ms:
CMake Error: The source "/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_omp_r2/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt" does not match the source "/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_hybrid_r1/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt" used to generate cache.  Re-run cmake with a different source directory.

+                    candidate_members, in_cluster);
+
+                // The serial implementation keeps the first seed on a tie.
+                // Compare the original position explicitly because dynamic
+                // scheduling does not define the order in which work arrives.
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality &&
+                     (local_best_position < 0 || i < local_best_position))) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                    thread_best_members[thread_id] = candidate_members;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_max_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp for makes every worker's
+            // result available before the deterministic serial reduction.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int max_cardinality = -1;
+                int best_position = -1;
+                int best_seed = -1;
+                std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+                for (int i = 0; i < max_threads; ++i) {
+                    const int position = thread_best_position[i];
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[i] == max_cardinality &&
+                         position >= 0 &&
+                         (best_position < 0 || position < best_position))) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[i];
+                        best_position = position;
+                        best_seed = position >= 0 ? unclustered_indices[position] : -1;
+                        best_cluster_members = thread_best_members[i];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. This is intentionally kept
+                // serial: it is the state transition that defines the next
+                // QT iteration and preserves the original semantics.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                           return clustered[index] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp single publishes the
+            // state transition before any worker starts the next iteration.
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;

+                         position >= 0 &&
+                         (best_position < 0 || position < best_position))) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[i];
+                        best_position = position;
+                        best_seed = position >= 0 ? unclustered_indices[position] : -1;
+                        best_cluster_members = thread_best_members[i];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. This is intentionally kept
+                // serial: it is the state transition that defines the next
+                // QT iteration and preserves the original semantics.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                           return clustered[index] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp single publishes the
+            // state transition before any worker starts the next iteration.
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
     
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<std::vector<int>> thread_best_members(max_threads);
+    bool done = false;
+
+    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
+    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent. Keep one OpenMP team
+    // alive for the whole algorithm to avoid repeatedly creating teams and to
+    // retain each worker's scratch buffers between iterations.
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, max_threads, clustered, \
+                                               unclustered_indices, clusters, \
+                                               thread_best_cardinality, \
+                                               thread_best_position, \
+                                               thread_best_members, done)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);
+        std::vector<int> candidate_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = -1;
+
+            // Try each unclustered point as a seed. Dynamic scheduling balances
+            // seeds whose candidate clusters require different amounts of work.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size()); ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
+                    candidate_members, in_cluster);
+
+                // The serial implementation keeps the first seed on a tie.
+                // Compare the original position explicitly because dynamic
+                // scheduling does not define the order in which work arrives.
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality &&
+                     (local_best_position < 0 || i < local_best_position))) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                    thread_best_members[thread_id] = candidate_members;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_max_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp for makes every worker's
+            // result available before the deterministic serial reduction.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int max_cardinality = -1;
+                int best_position = -1;
+                int best_seed = -1;
+                std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+                for (int i = 0; i < max_threads; ++i) {
+                    const int position = thread_best_position[i];
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[i] == max_cardinality &&
+                         position >= 0 &&
+                         (best_position < 0 || position < best_position))) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[i];
+                        best_position = position;
+                        best_seed = position >= 0 ? unclustered_indices[position] : -1;
+                        best_cluster_members = thread_best_members[i];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. This is intentionally kept
+                // serial: it is the state transition that defines the next
+                // QT iteration and preserves the original semantics.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                           return clustered[index] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp single publishes the
+            // state transition before any worker starts the next iteration.
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
     
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<std::vector<int>> thread_best_members(max_threads);
+    bool done = false;
+
+    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
+    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent. Keep one OpenMP team
+    // alive for the whole algorithm to avoid repeatedly creating teams and to
+    // retain each worker's scratch buffers between iterations.
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, max_threads, clustered, \
+                                               unclustered_indices, clusters, \
+                                               thread_best_cardinality, \
+                                               thread_best_position, \
+                                               thread_best_members, done)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);
+        std::vector<int> candidate_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = -1;
+
+            // Try each unclustered point as a seed. Dynamic scheduling balances
+            // seeds whose candidate clusters require different amounts of work.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size()); ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
+                    candidate_members, in_cluster);
+
+                // The serial implementation keeps the first seed on a tie.
+                // Compare the original position explicitly because dynamic
+                // scheduling does not define the order in which work arrives.
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality &&
+                     (local_best_position < 0 || i < local_best_position))) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                    thread_best_members[thread_id] = candidate_members;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_max_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp for makes every worker's
+            // result available before the deterministic serial reduction.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int max_cardinality = -1;
+                int best_position = -1;
+                int best_seed = -1;
+                std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+                for (int i = 0; i < max_threads; ++i) {
+                    const int position = thread_best_position[i];
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[i] == max_cardinality &&
+                         position >= 0 &&
+                         (best_position < 0 || position < best_position))) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[i];
+                        best_position = position;
+                        best_seed = position >= 0 ? unclustered_indices[position] : -1;
+                        best_cluster_members = thread_best_members[i];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. This is intentionally kept
+                // serial: it is the state transition that defines the next
+                // QT iteration and preserves the original semantics.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                           return clustered[index] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp single publishes the
+            // state transition before any worker starts the next iteration.
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
     
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<std::vector<int>> thread_best_members(max_threads);
+    bool done = false;
+
+    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
+    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent. Keep one OpenMP team
+    // alive for the whole algorithm to avoid repeatedly creating teams and to
+    // retain each worker's scratch buffers between iterations.
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, max_threads, clustered, \
+                                               unclustered_indices, clusters, \
+                                               thread_best_cardinality, \
+                                               thread_best_position, \
+                                               thread_best_members, done)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);
+        std::vector<int> candidate_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = -1;
+
+            // Try each unclustered point as a seed. Dynamic scheduling balances
+            // seeds whose candidate clusters require different amounts of work.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size()); ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
+                    candidate_members, in_cluster);
+
+                // The serial implementation keeps the first seed on a tie.
+                // Compare the original position explicitly because dynamic
+                // scheduling does not define the order in which work arrives.
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality &&
+                     (local_best_position < 0 || i < local_best_position))) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                    thread_best_members[thread_id] = candidate_members;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_max_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp for makes every worker's
+            // result available before the deterministic serial reduction.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int max_cardinality = -1;
+                int best_position = -1;
+                int best_seed = -1;
+                std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+                for (int i = 0; i < max_threads; ++i) {
+                    const int position = thread_best_position[i];
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[i] == max_cardinality &&
+                         position >= 0 &&
+                         (best_position < 0 || position < best_position))) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[i];
+                        best_position = position;
+                        best_seed = position >= 0 ? unclustered_indices[position] : -1;
+                        best_cluster_members = thread_best_members[i];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. This is intentionally kept
+                // serial: it is the state transition that defines the next
+                // QT iteration and preserves the original semantics.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                           return clustered[index] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp single publishes the
+            // state transition before any worker starts the next iteration.
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
-    }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    return static_cast<int>(cluster_members.size());

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
     
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<std::vector<int>> thread_best_members(max_threads);
+    bool done = false;
+
+    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
+    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent. Keep one OpenMP team
+    // alive for the whole algorithm to avoid repeatedly creating teams and to
+    // retain each worker's scratch buffers between iterations.
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, max_threads, clustered, \
+                                               unclustered_indices, clusters, \
+                                               thread_best_cardinality, \
+                                               thread_best_position, \
+                                               thread_best_members, done)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);
+        std::vector<int> candidate_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = -1;
+
+            // Try each unclustered point as a seed. Dynamic scheduling balances
+            // seeds whose candidate clusters require different amounts of work.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size()); ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
+                    candidate_members, in_cluster);
+
+                // The serial implementation keeps the first seed on a tie.
+                // Compare the original position explicitly because dynamic
+                // scheduling does not define the order in which work arrives.
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality &&
+                     (local_best_position < 0 || i < local_best_position))) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                    thread_best_members[thread_id] = candidate_members;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_max_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp for makes every worker's
+            // result available before the deterministic serial reduction.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int max_cardinality = -1;
+                int best_position = -1;
+                int best_seed = -1;
+                std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+                for (int i = 0; i < max_threads; ++i) {
+                    const int position = thread_best_position[i];
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[i] == max_cardinality &&
+                         position >= 0 &&
+                         (best_position < 0 || position < best_position))) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[i];
+                        best_position = position;
+                        best_seed = position >= 0 ? unclustered_indices[position] : -1;
+                        best_cluster_members = thread_best_members[i];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. This is intentionally kept
+                // serial: it is the state transition that defines the next
+                // QT iteration and preserves the original semantics.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                           return clustered[index] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp single publishes the
+            // state transition before any worker starts the next iteration.
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }
