+                // The serial implementation keeps the first seed on a tie.
+                // Compare the original position explicitly because dynamic
+                // scheduling does not define the order in which work arrives.
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality &&
+                     (local_best_position < 0 || i < local_best_position))) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                    thread_best_members[thread_id] = candidate_members;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_max_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp for makes every worker's
+            // result available before the deterministic serial reduction.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int max_cardinality = -1;
+                int best_position = -1;
+                int best_seed = -1;
+                std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+                for (int i = 0; i < max_threads; ++i) {
+                    const int position = thread_best_position[i];
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[i] == max_cardinality &&
+                         position >= 0 &&
+                         (best_position < 0 || position < best_position))) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[i];
+                        best_position = position;
+                        best_seed = position >= 0 ? unclustered_indices[position] : -1;
+                        best_cluster_members = thread_best_members[i];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. This is intentionally kept
+                // serial: it is the state transition that defines the next
+                // QT iteration and preserves the original semantics.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                           return clustered[index] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            // The implicit barrier at the end of omp single publishes the
+            // state transition before any worker starts the next iteration.
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }
 

codex
The first persistent-team version exposed a nondeterministic result under multiple threads, so I’m isolating that synchronization issue before proceeding. The serial output still matches exactly, but parallel correctness is non-negotiable; I’m switching the reduction to explicit per-seed result slots temporarily so each candidate’s data has an unambiguous owner, then I’ll re-check scalability and trim any avoidable overhead.

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
     
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
+    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
+    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent.
     while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+        const int unclustered_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+        std::vector<int> cardinalities(unclustered_count, 0);
+        std::vector<std::vector<int>> candidate_members(unclustered_count);
+
+        #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, clustered, \
+                                                   unclustered_indices, \
+                                                   unclustered_count, \
+                                                   cardinalities, candidate_members)
+        {
+            std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);
+
+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose candidate clusters
+            // require different amounts of work.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                cardinalities[i] = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
+                    candidate_members[i], in_cluster);
+            }
+        }
+
         int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+        int best_position = -1;
+
+        // This serial reduction preserves the original first-on-tie rule.
+        for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
+            if (cardinalities[i] > max_cardinality) {
+                max_cardinality = cardinalities[i];
+                best_position = i;
             }
         }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+
+        // If we found a cluster, add it. The state transition remains serial
+        // so the next QT iteration sees exactly the selected prior state.
+        if (best_position >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
             Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+            cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_position];
+            cluster.members = std::move(candidate_members[best_position]);
+            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+            for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                clustered[member] = 1;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
+
             unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
+                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                               unclustered_indices.end(),
+                               [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                   return clustered[index] != 0;
+                               }),
+                unclustered_indices.end());
         } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
+            // No more clusters can be formed.
             break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<unsigned char>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);

            // The maximum can only increase as more members are checked.  A
            // candidate that has already crossed either bound cannot become
            // the best candidate, so avoid the remaining distance calls.
            if (max_dist >= threshold || max_dist >= min_diameter) break;
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                              std::vector<unsigned char>& in_cluster) {
    std::fill(in_cluster.begin(), in_cluster.end(), 0);
    cluster_members.clear();
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    cluster_members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(cluster_members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(cluster_members, clustered, in_cluster, points,
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        cluster_members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(cluster_members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent.
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        const int unclustered_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
        std::vector<int> cardinalities(unclustered_count, 0);
        std::vector<std::vector<int>> candidate_members(unclustered_count);

        #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, clustered, \
                                                   unclustered_indices, \
                                                   unclustered_count, \
                                                   cardinalities, candidate_members)
        {
            std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);

            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose candidate clusters
            // require different amounts of work.
            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
            for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
                cardinalities[i] = generateCandidateCluster(
                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
                    candidate_members[i], in_cluster);
            }
        }

        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_position = -1;

        // This serial reduction preserves the original first-on-tie rule.
        for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
            if (cardinalities[i] > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinalities[i];
                best_position = i;
            }
        }

        // If we found a cluster, add it. The state transition remains serial
        // so the next QT iteration sees exactly the selected prior state.
        if (best_position >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_position];
            cluster.members = std::move(candidate_members[best_position]);
            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));

            for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
                clustered[member] = 1;
            }

            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
                               unclustered_indices.end(),
                               [&clustered](const int index) {
                                   return clustered[index] != 0;
                               }),
                unclustered_indices.end());
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed.
            break;
        }
    }

    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
    }
    
    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;

     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
+    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
+    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent.

-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
+        const int unclustered_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+        std::vector<int> cardinalities(unclustered_count, 0);

+            // require different amounts of work.

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
     
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
+    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
+    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent.
     while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
+        const int unclustered_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+        std::vector<int> cardinalities(unclustered_count, 0);
+
+        #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, clustered, \
+                                                   unclustered_indices, \
+                                                   unclustered_count, cardinalities)
+        {
+            std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);
             std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose candidate clusters
+            // require different amounts of work.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                cardinalities[i] = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
+                    candidate_members[i], in_cluster);
             }
         }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+
+        int max_cardinality = -1;
+        int best_position = -1;
+
+        // This serial reduction preserves the original first-on-tie rule.
+        for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
+            if (cardinalities[i] > max_cardinality) {
+                max_cardinality = cardinalities[i];
+                best_position = i;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // If we found a cluster, add it. The state transition remains serial
+        // so the next QT iteration sees exactly the selected prior state.
+        if (best_position >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+            // Candidate members are needed only for the winning seed. Rebuild
+            // that one candidate after the cardinality reduction, avoiding an
+            // O(N) result vector for every seed while retaining the original
+            // deterministic selection rule.
+            std::vector<unsigned char> best_in_cluster(N, 0);
+            std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+            generateCandidateCluster(
+                unclustered_indices[best_position], clustered, points,
+                threshold, N, best_cluster_members, best_in_cluster);
+
             Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+            cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_position];
+            cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+            for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                clustered[member] = 1;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
+
             unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
+                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                               unclustered_indices.end(),
+                               [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                   return clustered[index] != 0;
+                               }),
+                unclustered_indices.end());
         } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
+            // No more clusters can be formed.
             break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
     
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
+    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
+    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent.
     while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
+        const int unclustered_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+        std::vector<int> cardinalities(unclustered_count, 0);
+
+        #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, clustered, \
+                                                   unclustered_indices, \
+                                                   unclustered_count, cardinalities)
+        {
+            std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);
             std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose candidate clusters
+            // require different amounts of work.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                cardinalities[i] = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
+                    candidate_members, in_cluster);
             }
         }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+
+        int max_cardinality = -1;
+        int best_position = -1;
+
+        // This serial reduction preserves the original first-on-tie rule.
+        for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
+            if (cardinalities[i] > max_cardinality) {
+                max_cardinality = cardinalities[i];
+                best_position = i;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // If we found a cluster, add it. The state transition remains serial
+        // so the next QT iteration sees exactly the selected prior state.
+        if (best_position >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+            // Candidate members are needed only for the winning seed. Rebuild
+            // that one candidate after the cardinality reduction, avoiding an
+            // O(N) result vector for every seed while retaining the original
+            // deterministic selection rule.
+            std::vector<unsigned char> best_in_cluster(N, 0);
+            std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+            generateCandidateCluster(
+                unclustered_indices[best_position], clustered, points,
+                threshold, N, best_cluster_members, best_in_cluster);
+
             Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+            cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_position];
+            cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+            for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                clustered[member] = 1;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
+
             unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
+                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                               unclustered_indices.end(),
+                               [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                   return clustered[index] != 0;
+                               }),
+                unclustered_indices.end());
         } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
+            // No more clusters can be formed.
             break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

  printf 'omp: '; echo \""'$par_out" | tr '"'\\n' ' '; echo

done' in /home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_omp_r2

codex
The explicit per-seed reduction is now deterministic across repeated runs and matches the original membership hash across several sizes and thresholds. I reduced its memory footprint as well: workers retain only scratch buffers and cardinalities; the full member list is regenerated only for the winning seed before the serial state update.

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
     
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
+    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
+    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent.
     while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+        const int unclustered_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+        std::vector<int> cardinalities(unclustered_count, 0);
+
+        #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, clustered, \
+                                                   unclustered_indices, \
+                                                   unclustered_count, cardinalities)
+        {
+            std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);
+            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
+
+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose candidate clusters
+            // require different amounts of work.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                cardinalities[i] = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
+                    candidate_members, in_cluster);
+            }
+        }
+
         int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+        int best_position = -1;
+
+        // This serial reduction preserves the original first-on-tie rule.
+        for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
+            if (cardinalities[i] > max_cardinality) {
+                max_cardinality = cardinalities[i];
+                best_position = i;
             }
         }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+
+        // If we found a cluster, add it. The state transition remains serial
+        // so the next QT iteration sees exactly the selected prior state.
+        if (best_position >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+            // Candidate members are needed only for the winning seed. Rebuild
+            // that one candidate after the cardinality reduction, avoiding an
+            // O(N) result vector for every seed while retaining the original
+            // deterministic selection rule.
+            std::vector<unsigned char> best_in_cluster(N, 0);
+            std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+            generateCandidateCluster(
+                unclustered_indices[best_position], clustered, points,
+                threshold, N, best_cluster_members, best_in_cluster);
+
             Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+            cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_position];
+            cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
+            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+            for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                clustered[member] = 1;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
+
             unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
+                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                               unclustered_indices.end(),
+                               [&clustered](const int index) {
+                                   return clustered[index] != 0;
+                               }),
+                unclustered_indices.end());
         } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
+            // No more clusters can be formed.
             break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;

+            cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);

     1	// QT Clustering Benchmark - OpenMP Parallel Version
     2	// 
     3	// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
     4	// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
     5	// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.
     6	
     7	#include <algorithm>
     8	#include <chrono>
     9	#include <cmath>
    10	#include <cstdio>
    11	#include <cstdlib>
    12	#include <cstring>
    13	#include <limits>
    14	#include <utility>
    15	#include <vector>
    16	
    17	#include <omp.h>
    18	
    19	#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
    20	
    21	static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
    22	static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;
    23	
    24	// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
    25	struct Point {
    26	    double x, y;
    27	};
    28	
    29	// Structure to represent a cluster
    30	struct Cluster {
    31	    std::vector<int> members;
    32	    int seed_point;
    33	};
    34	
    35	// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
    36	void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    37	    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    38	    
    39	    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    40	    int count = 0;
    41	    
    42	    while (count < N) {
    43	        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
    44	        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
    45	        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
    46	        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
    47	        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
    48	        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
    49	        
    50	        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
    51	        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
    52	            group_cnt = N - count;
    53	        }
    54	        
    55	        while (group_cnt > 0) {
    56	            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
    57	            const double r = frand() * R;
    58	            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
    59	            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
    60	            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
    61	            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
    62	            
    63	            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
    64	                continue;
    65	            }
    66	            
    67	            points[count] = {x, y};
    68	            count++;
    69	            group_cnt--;
    70	        }
    71	    }
    72	}
    73	
    74	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
    75	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    76	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    77	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    78	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    79	}
    80	
    81	// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
    82	// Returns -1 if no such point exists
    83	int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
    84	                     const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
    85	                     const std::vector<unsigned char>& in_cluster,
    86	                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
    87	                     const double threshold,
    88	                     const int point_count) {
    89	    int closest_point = -1;
    90	    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    91	    
    92	    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    93	    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
    94	        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
    95	        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
    96	        
    97	        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
    98	        double max_dist = 0.0;
    99	        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
   100	            const int member = cluster_members[i];
   101	            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
   102	            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
   103	
   104	            // The maximum can only increase as more members are checked.  A
   105	            // candidate that has already crossed either bound cannot become
   106	            // the best candidate, so avoid the remaining distance calls.
   107	            if (max_dist >= threshold || max_dist >= min_diameter) break;
   108	        }
   109	        
   110	        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
   111	        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
   112	            min_diameter = max_dist;
   113	            closest_point = candidate;
   114	        }
   115	    }
   116	    
   117	    return closest_point;
   118	}
   119	
   120	// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
   121	// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
   122	int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
   123	                              const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
   124	                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
   125	                              const double threshold,
   126	                              const int point_count,
   127	                              std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
   128	                              std::vector<unsigned char>& in_cluster) {
   129	    std::fill(in_cluster.begin(), in_cluster.end(), 0);
   130	    cluster_members.clear();
   131	    
   132	    // Add seed point
   133	    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
   134	    cluster_members.push_back(seed_point);
   135	    
   136	    // Iteratively add closest points
   137	    while (static_cast<int>(cluster_members.size()) < point_count) {
   138	        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
   139	        const int closest = findClosestPoint(cluster_members, clustered, in_cluster, points,
   140	                                             threshold, point_count);
   141	        
   142	        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
   143	        
   144	        in_cluster[closest] = true;
   145	        cluster_members.push_back(closest);
   146	    }
   147	    
   148	    return static_cast<int>(cluster_members.size());
   149	}
   150	
   151	// Main QT clustering algorithm
   152	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   153	                                  const double threshold) {
   154	    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   155	    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
   156	    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
   157	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   158	    
   159	    // Initialize unclustered indices
   160	    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
   161	    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
   162	        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
   163	    }
   164	    
   165	    // The selected cluster depends on the previous iteration, but every
   166	    // candidate seed in an iteration is independent.
   167	    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
   168	        const int unclustered_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
   169	        std::vector<int> cardinalities(unclustered_count, 0);
   170	
   171	        #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(points, threshold, N, clustered, \
   172	                                                   unclustered_indices, \
   173	                                                   unclustered_count, cardinalities)
   174	        {
   175	            std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(N, 0);
   176	            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
   177	
   178	            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose candidate clusters
   179	            // require different amounts of work.
   180	            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
   181	            for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
   182	                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
   183	                cardinalities[i] = generateCandidateCluster(
   184	                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
   185	                    candidate_members, in_cluster);
   186	            }
   187	        }
   188	
   189	        int max_cardinality = -1;
   190	        int best_position = -1;
   191	
   192	        // This serial reduction preserves the original first-on-tie rule.
   193	        for (int i = 0; i < unclustered_count; ++i) {
   194	            if (cardinalities[i] > max_cardinality) {
   195	                max_cardinality = cardinalities[i];
   196	                best_position = i;
   197	            }
   198	        }
   199	
   200	        // If we found a cluster, add it. The state transition remains serial
   201	        // so the next QT iteration sees exactly the selected prior state.
   202	        if (best_position >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
   203	            // Candidate members are needed only for the winning seed. Rebuild
   204	            // that one candidate after the cardinality reduction, avoiding an
   205	            // O(N) result vector for every seed while retaining the original
   206	            // deterministic selection rule.
   207	            std::vector<unsigned char> best_in_cluster(N, 0);
   208	            std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
   209	            generateCandidateCluster(
   210	                unclustered_indices[best_position], clustered, points,
   211	                threshold, N, best_cluster_members, best_in_cluster);
   212	
   213	            Cluster cluster;
   214	            cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_position];
   215	            cluster.members = std::move(best_cluster_members);
   216	            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
   217	
   218	            for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
   219	                clustered[member] = 1;
   220	            }
   221	
   222	            unclustered_indices.erase(
   223	                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
   224	                               unclustered_indices.end(),
   225	                               [&clustered](const int index) {
